</p><p>A teaser for the event had the title "The spotlight turns to notebooks."<sup id="cite&#95;ref-88" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-88"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>88<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2009">2009</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=72" title="Edit section: 2009"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Macworld_SF_(January_6,_2009)"><span id="Macworld_SF_.28January_6.2C_2009.29"></span>Macworld SF (January 6, 2009)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=73" title="Edit section: Macworld SF (January 6, 2009)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Phil Schiller presented the event. Steve Jobs was not at the event.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-CNETMacworld2009&#95;89-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CNETMacworld2009-89"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>89<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>Phil Schiller announced that the iTunes Store would start to sell DRM-free tracks.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-CNETMacworld2009&#95;89-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CNETMacworld2009-89"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>89<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li>
<li>The 17-inch unibody Macbook Pro was announced.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-CNETMacworld2009&#95;89-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CNETMacworld2009-89"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>89<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li>
<li>iLife '09 and iWork '09 were announced.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-CNETMacworld2009&#95;89-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CNETMacworld2009-89"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>89<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2009_(June_8–12,_2009)"><span id="WWDC_2009_.28June_8.E2.80.9312.2C_2009.29"></span>WWDC 2009 (June 8–12, 2009)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=74" title="Edit section: WWDC 2009 (June 8–12, 2009)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1305433154" /><table class="box-Unreferenced&#95;section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img alt="icon" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/60px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/120px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></span></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a href="/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events" title="Special:EditPage/List of Apple Inc. media events">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">June 2024</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>Announcements at the keynote included the release of the <a href="/wiki/IOS_version_history" title="IOS version history">iPhone OS 3.0</a> software announced to developers in March, a demonstration of <a href="/wiki/Mac_OS_X_Snow_Leopard" title="Mac OS X Snow Leopard">Mac OS X Snow Leopard</a> (10.6), the new 13" <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Pro" title="MacBook Pro">MacBook Pro</a>, updates to the 15" and 17" MacBook Pros, and the new <a href="/wiki/IPhone_3GS" title="IPhone 3GS">iPhone 3GS</a>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(September_9,_2009)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28September_9.2C_2009.29"></span>Apple special event (September 9, 2009)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=75" title="Edit section: Apple special event (September 9, 2009)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1305433154" /><table class="box-Unreferenced&#95;section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img alt="icon" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/60px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/120px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></span></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a href="/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events" title="Special:EditPage/List of Apple Inc. media events">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">June 2024</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>Announcements at the keynote included the release of <a href="/wiki/ITunes" title="ITunes">iTunes</a> 9, ability to purchase Ringtones for <a href="/wiki/IPhone" title="IPhone">iPhone</a>, <a href="/wiki/ITunes_LP" title="ITunes LP">iTunes LP</a>, the <a href="/wiki/IPod_Nano#5th_generation" title="IPod Nano">5th-generation iPod nano</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPod_Touch_(3rd_generation)" title="IPod Touch (3rd generation)">3rd-generation iPod touch</a>, and a thinner 160GB <a href="/wiki/IPod_Classic#6th_generation" title="IPod Classic">6th-generation iPod classic</a>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2010">2010</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=76" title="Edit section: 2010"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(January_27,_2010)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28January_27.2C_2010.29"></span>Apple special event (January 27, 2010)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=77" title="Edit section: Apple special event (January 27, 2010)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>The <a href="/wiki/IPad_(1st_generation)" title="IPad (1st generation)">iPad</a> was announced on January 27, 2010, by <a href="/wiki/Steve_Jobs" title="Steve Jobs">Steve Jobs</a> at an Apple press conference at the <a href="/wiki/Yerba_Buena_Center_for_the_Arts" title="Yerba Buena Center for the Arts">Yerba Buena Center for the Arts</a> in San Francisco.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-Apple&#95;PR-Jan&#95;90-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Apple_PR-Jan-90"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>90<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-91" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-91"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>91<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Jobs later said that Apple began developing the <a href="/wiki/IPad" title="IPad">iPad</a> before the <a href="/wiki/IPhone" title="IPhone">iPhone</a>,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-keynoteMacworld&#95;92-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-keynoteMacworld-92"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>92<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-keynoteEngadget&#95;93-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-keynoteEngadget-93"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>93<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-levgrossman&#95;94-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-levgrossman-94"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>94<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> but temporarily shelved the effort upon realizing that its ideas would work just as well in a mobile phone.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-95" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-95"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>95<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The iPad's internal codename was K48, which was revealed in the court case surrounding leaking of iPad information before launch.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-96" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-96"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>96<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(April_8,_2010)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28April_8.2C_2010.29"></span>Apple special event (April 8, 2010)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=78" title="Edit section: Apple special event (April 8, 2010)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Steve Jobs presented the event. Apple revealed <a href="/wiki/IOS_4" title="IOS 4">iOS 4</a> (then known as iPhone OS 4).<sup id="cite&#95;ref-97" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-97"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>97<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> In Apple's description, it includes "over 100 new user features for <a href="/wiki/IPhone" title="IPhone">iPhone</a> and <a href="/wiki/IPod_Touch" title="IPod Touch">iPod Touch</a> owners to enjoy. And for developers, a new software development kit (SDK) offers over 1500 new APIs to create apps that are even more powerful, innovative, and amazing."<sup id="cite&#95;ref-apple-events-podcast&#95;3-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-apple-events-podcast-3"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>3<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2010_(June_7–11,_2010)"><span id="WWDC_2010_.28June_7.E2.80.9311.2C_2010.29"></span>WWDC 2010 (June 7–11, 2010)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=79" title="Edit section: WWDC 2010 (June 7–11, 2010)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1305433154" /><table class="box-Unreferenced&#95;section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img alt="icon" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/60px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/120px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></span></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a href="/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events" title="Special:EditPage/List of Apple Inc. media events">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">April 2024</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>On June 7, 2010, Jobs announced the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_4" title="IPhone 4">iPhone 4</a>. Also, iPhone OS was renamed to <a href="/wiki/IOS" title="IOS">iOS</a>. The <a href="/wiki/FaceTime" title="FaceTime">FaceTime</a> and <a href="/wiki/IMovie" title="IMovie">iMovie</a> app for iPhone applications were also announced.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(September_1,_2010)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28September_1.2C_2010.29"></span>Apple special event (September 1, 2010)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=80" title="Edit section: Apple special event (September 1, 2010)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1305433154" /><table class="box-Unreferenced&#95;section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img alt="icon" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/60px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/120px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></span></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a href="/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events" title="Special:EditPage/List of Apple Inc. media events">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">April 2024</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>Steve Jobs opened iOS 4.1, iOS 4.2 Preview, 4th gen Shuffle, 6th gen Nano, 4th gen iPod Touch, iTunes 10 with Ping. and 2nd gen Apple TV.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple's_'Back_to_the_Mac'_event_(October_20,_2010)"><span id="Apple.27s_.27Back_to_the_Mac.27_event_.28October_20.2C_2010.29"></span>Apple's 'Back to the Mac' event (October 20, 2010)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=81" title="Edit section: Apple&#039;s &#039;Back to the Mac&#039; event (October 20, 2010)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>A preview of <a href="/wiki/Mac_OS_X_Lion" class="mw-redirect" title="Mac OS X Lion">Mac OS X Lion</a> was shown, featuring updated design elements that took inspiration from the iPad.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-98" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-98"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>98<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Steve Jobs opened the event.
</p><p>iLife '11 was announced, featuring updated versions iPhoto, iMovie, and Garageband. Gerhard Lengeling, Senior Director of Music Creation Applications at Apple helped to give a demo of the new version of Garageband. iLife '11 was announced to be free with every new Mac, and $49 for existing Macs.
</p><p>Attention was given to FaceTime; Steve Jobs announced that Apple had shipped 19 million FaceTime-capable devices (iPod touches and iPhones) since June 2010, when FaceTime was first launched. FaceTime for the Mac was announced.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2011">2011</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=82" title="Edit section: 2011"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(March_2,_2011)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28March_2.2C_2011.29"></span>Apple special event (March 2, 2011)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=83" title="Edit section: Apple special event (March 2, 2011)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Apple sent invitations to journalists on February 23, 2011, for a media event on March 2.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-Feb.&#95;23&#95;invite&#95;99-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Feb._23_invite-99"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>99<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Apple CEO <a href="/wiki/Steve_Jobs" title="Steve Jobs">Steve Jobs</a> revealed the <a href="/wiki/IPad_2" title="IPad 2">iPad 2</a> device at the <a href="/wiki/Yerba_Buena_Center_for_the_Arts" title="Yerba Buena Center for the Arts">Yerba Buena Center for the Arts</a> on March 2, 2011, despite being on medical leave.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-100" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-100"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>100<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-PCWorldiOS43&#95;101-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-PCWorldiOS43-101"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>101<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2011_(June_6–10,_2011)"><span id="WWDC_2011_.28June_6.E2.80.9310.2C_2011.29"></span>WWDC 2011 (June 6–10, 2011)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=84" title="Edit section: WWDC 2011 (June 6–10, 2011)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1305433154" /><table class="box-Unreferenced&#95;section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img alt="icon" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/60px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/120px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></span></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a href="/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events" title="Special:EditPage/List of Apple Inc. media events">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">April 2024</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>During the WWDC keynote, Apple announced the release of <a href="/wiki/Mac_OS_X_Lion" class="mw-redirect" title="Mac OS X Lion">Mac OS X Lion</a> the following July and <a href="/wiki/IOS_5" title="IOS 5">iOS 5</a> in the fall of 2011, as well as the launch of the cloud service <a href="/wiki/ICloud" title="ICloud">iCloud</a> as a replacement for <a href="/wiki/MobileMe" title="MobileMe">MobileMe</a>, and the <a href="/wiki/ITunes#iTunes_in_the_Cloud_and_iTunes_Match" title="ITunes">iTunes Match</a> subscription.
</p><p>This keynote was not streamed live.<sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;">&#91;<i><a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (July 2023)">citation needed</span></a></i>&#93;</sup>
</p><p>Steve Jobs opened the event. Phil Schiller covered new features of Mac OS X Lion, such as new multi-touch gestures and a change in how full-screen applications could be handled. It was the last event to be held by Steve Jobs.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(October_4,_2011)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28October_4.2C_2011.29"></span>Apple special event (October 4, 2011)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=85" title="Edit section: Apple special event (October 4, 2011)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1305433154" /><table class="box-Unreferenced&#95;section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img alt="icon" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/60px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/120px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></span></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a href="/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events" title="Special:EditPage/List of Apple Inc. media events">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">April 2024</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>On October 4, 2011, Apple held a media event in which it introduced <a href="/wiki/Find_My_Friends" class="mw-redirect" title="Find My Friends">Find My Friends</a> and refreshed <a href="/wiki/IPod_Nano" title="IPod Nano">iPod Nano</a> and <a href="/wiki/IPod_Touch" title="IPod Touch">iPod Touch</a> models, and revealed the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_4s" title="IPhone 4s">iPhone 4S</a> with the <a href="/wiki/Siri_(software)" class="mw-redirect" title="Siri (software)">Siri</a> voice assistant as its key feature. The event was led by <a href="/wiki/Tim_Cook" title="Tim Cook">Tim Cook</a> following Jobs’s resignation as CEO on August 24.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-102" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-102"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>102<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2012">2012</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=86" title="Edit section: 2012"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(March_7,_2012)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28March_7.2C_2012.29"></span>Apple special event (March 7, 2012)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=87" title="Edit section: Apple special event (March 7, 2012)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>On February 28, 2012, Apple announced a media event scheduled for March 7, 2012, at the Yerba Buena Center. Apple didn't disclose in advance what would be announced at the event, but it was widely expected to be a new version of the <a href="/wiki/IPad" title="IPad">iPad</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-103" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-103"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>103<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-104" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-104"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>104<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-CNET&#95;105-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CNET-105"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>105<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-106" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-106"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>106<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>  It was also rumored that Apple might release a new <a href="/wiki/Television" title="Television">television</a> <a href="/wiki/Set_top_box" class="mw-redirect" title="Set top box">set top box</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-107" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-107"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>107<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>  The announcement affected the tablet resale market,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-108" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-108"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>108<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> and Apple's stock price reached a record closing figure on the same day that the <a href="/wiki/Dow_Jones_Industrial_Average" title="Dow Jones Industrial Average">Dow Jones Industrial Average</a> reached a closing figure of above 13,000 for the first time since the <a href="/wiki/2008_financial_crisis" title="2008 financial crisis">2008 financial crisis</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-109" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-109"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>109<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>  (Apple is not a Dow Jones component.)
</p><p>The keynote began 10 AM PST (18:00&#160;UTC) with Cook introducing <a href="/wiki/IOS_version_history#iOS_5" title="IOS version history">iOS 5.1</a>, a Japanese version of <a href="/wiki/Siri_(software)" class="mw-redirect" title="Siri (software)">Siri</a>, and the 3rd generation <a href="/wiki/Apple_TV_(device)" title="Apple TV (device)">Apple TV</a>. The key announcement was the introduction of the <a href="/wiki/IPad_(3rd_generation)" title="IPad (3rd generation)">third-generation iPad</a> that featured a high-resolution <a href="/wiki/Retina_display" title="Retina display">Retina display</a> and a marketing tagline “Resolutionary” as a wordplay.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-110" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-110"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>110<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Eddy_Cue" title="Eddy Cue">Eddy Cue</a> gave a demo of the new Apple TV interface. At the media event, Cook talked about a 'post-PC world', a world where the personal computer is no longer the center of one's digital life, and of how the 3rd generation iPad will be one of the main contributors of the 'post-PC world'.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-111" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-111"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>111<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2012_(June_11–15,_2012)"><span id="WWDC_2012_.28June_11.E2.80.9315.2C_2012.29"></span>WWDC 2012 (June 11–15, 2012)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=88" title="Edit section: WWDC 2012 (June 11–15, 2012)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1305433154" /><table class="box-Unreferenced&#95;section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img alt="icon" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/60px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/120px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></span></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a href="/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events" title="Special:EditPage/List of Apple Inc. media events">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">April 2024</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>WWDC 2012 was held in Moscone Center West. The ticket price remained the same as the 2010 WWDC, selling at US$1,599. Apple changed the purchasing process by requiring purchases to be made using an Apple ID associated with a paid Apple developer account. Tickets went on sale shortly after 8:30&#160;am Eastern Time on April 25, 2012, and were sold out within 1 hour and 43 minutes.
</p><p>The keynote on June 11 highlighted the launch of <a href="/wiki/Apple_Maps" title="Apple Maps">Apple Maps</a>, and also announced refreshed models of the <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Air" title="MacBook Air">MacBook Air</a> and <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Pro" title="MacBook Pro">MacBook Pro</a>, and the all-new 15-inch MacBook Pro with <a href="/wiki/Retina_Display" class="mw-redirect" title="Retina Display">Retina Display</a>. Apple also showcased <a href="/wiki/OS_X_Mountain_Lion" title="OS X Mountain Lion">OS X Mountain Lion</a> (released in July 2012) and <a href="/wiki/IOS_6" title="IOS 6">iOS 6</a> (released in September 2012).
</p><p>This keynote was streamed live exclusively on iOS and OS X devices, through the <a href="/wiki/Safari_(web_browser)" title="Safari (web browser)">Safari</a> web browser.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(September_12,_2012)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28September_12.2C_2012.29"></span>Apple special event (September 12, 2012)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=89" title="Edit section: Apple special event (September 12, 2012)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1305433154" /><table class="box-Unreferenced&#95;section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img alt="icon" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/60px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/120px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></span></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a href="/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events" title="Special:EditPage/List of Apple Inc. media events">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">April 2024</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p><a href="/wiki/Phil_Schiller" title="Phil Schiller">Phil Schiller</a>, Apple's senior vice president of worldwide marketing, took the wraps off the new <a href="/wiki/IPhone" title="IPhone">iPhone</a> for press gathered at the company's San Francisco event, calling the device "the most beautiful product we've ever made, bar none." The <a href="/wiki/IPhone_5" title="IPhone 5">iPhone 5</a> is made entirely of glass and aluminum, Schiller said, adding that the "exacting level of standards" exhibited by the phone is Apple's best hardware engineering to date.
</p><p>It's the thinnest and lightest iPhone, at 7.6mm thin, and 112 grams. Schiller said those measurements make it the world's thinnest smartphone. The <a href="/wiki/IPhone_5" title="IPhone 5">iPhone 5</a> was also volumetrically smaller than the previous model, the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_4S" class="mw-redirect" title="IPhone 4S">iPhone 4S</a>.<sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;">&#91;<i><a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (April 2023)">citation needed</span></a></i>&#93;</sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(October_23,_2012)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28October_23.2C_2012.29"></span>Apple special event (October 23, 2012)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=90" title="Edit section: Apple special event (October 23, 2012)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1305433154" /><table class="box-Unreferenced&#95;section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img alt="icon" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/60px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/120px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></span></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a href="/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events" title="Special:EditPage/List of Apple Inc. media events">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">April 2024</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>On October 23, 2012, Apple CEO Tim Cook unveiled the new <a href="/wiki/IPad_Mini_(1st_generation)" title="IPad Mini (1st generation)">iPad Mini</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPad_4" class="mw-redirect" title="IPad 4">fourth generation iPad</a> with Retina display, new <a href="/wiki/IMac" title="IMac">iMac</a>, and the 13-inch <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Pro" title="MacBook Pro">MacBook Pro</a> with Retina display.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2013">2013</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=91" title="Edit section: 2013"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2013_(June_10–14,_2013)"><span id="WWDC_2013_.28June_10.E2.80.9314.2C_2013.29"></span>WWDC 2013 (June 10–14, 2013)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=92" title="Edit section: WWDC 2013 (June 10–14, 2013)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>WWDC 2013 was held at Moscone West in San Francisco – the same venue as in previous years.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-engadget&#95;wwdc13&#95;announced&#95;112-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-engadget_wwdc13_announced-112"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>112<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-ars&#95;wwdc13&#95;announced&#95;113-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ars_wwdc13_announced-113"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>113<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Tickets went on sale at 10&#160;am <a href="/wiki/Pacific_Daylight_Time" class="mw-redirect" title="Pacific Daylight Time">PDT</a> on April 25, 2013,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-engadget&#95;wwdc13&#95;announced&#95;112-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-engadget_wwdc13_announced-112"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>112<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-ars&#95;wwdc13&#95;announced&#95;113-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ars_wwdc13_announced-113"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>113<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> selling out within 71 seconds (1 minute and 11 seconds).<sup id="cite&#95;ref-ars&#95;wwdc13&#95;sold&#95;out&#95;114-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ars_wwdc13_sold_out-114"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>114<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Apple also announced that it will award 150 free WWDC 2013 Student Scholarship tickets to those who want to attend in order to benefit from the conference's many workshops, with applications for a scholarship starting 9am PDT on April 29, 2013, and deadline slated for 5pm PDT on May 2, 2013.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-ars&#95;free&#95;tickets&#95;115-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ars_free_tickets-115"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>115<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Winning applicants were notified by May 16, 2013, though Apple states that it won't reimburse winners for travel or hotel expenses.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-ars&#95;free&#95;tickets&#95;115-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ars_free_tickets-115"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>115<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> In the keynote, Apple unveiled a redesigned model of the <a href="/wiki/Mac_Pro" title="Mac Pro">Mac Pro</a>, <a href="/wiki/AirPort_Time_Capsule" title="AirPort Time Capsule">AirPort Time Capsule</a>, and <a href="/wiki/AirPort_Extreme" title="AirPort Extreme">AirPort Extreme</a> as well as updated models of the <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Air" title="MacBook Air">MacBook Air</a>.  Apple also showcased <a href="/wiki/OS_X_Mavericks" title="OS X Mavericks">OS X Mavericks</a>, <a href="/wiki/IOS_7" title="IOS 7">iOS 7</a>, <a href="/wiki/IWork_for_iCloud" class="mw-redirect" title="IWork for iCloud">iWork for iCloud</a> and a new music streaming service named <a href="/wiki/ITunes_Radio" title="ITunes Radio">iTunes Radio</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-116" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-116"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>116<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Vampire_Weekend" title="Vampire Weekend">Vampire Weekend</a> performed at the Bash on June 13 at the Yerba Buena Gardens.
</p><p>This keynote was streamed live on June 10, 2013.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(September_10,_2013)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28September_10.2C_2013.29"></span>Apple special event (September 10, 2013)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=93" title="Edit section: Apple special event (September 10, 2013)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Apple announced the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_5C" class="mw-redirect" title="IPhone 5C">iPhone&#160;5C</a> and <a href="/wiki/IPhone_5S" class="mw-redirect" title="IPhone 5S">iPhone&#160;5S</a> during a media event called "This should brighten everyone's day."<sup id="cite&#95;ref-cnet-sept-2013-event-invitations&#95;117-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-cnet-sept-2013-event-invitations-117"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>117<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> at its <a href="/wiki/Cupertino" class="mw-redirect" title="Cupertino">Cupertino</a> headquarters on September 10, 2013.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-techradar&#95;confirmed&#95;5S&#95;release&#95;1&#95;118-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-techradar_confirmed_5S_release_1-118"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>118<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-verge&#95;5S&#95;unveil&#95;119-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-verge_5S_unveil-119"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>119<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> While the iPhone 5C became available for preorder on September 13, 2013, the iPhone&#160;5S first became available on September 20, 2013.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-120" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-120"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>120<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> While most of the promotion focused on <a href="/wiki/Touch_ID" title="Touch ID">Touch ID</a>, the 64-bit <a href="/wiki/Apple_A7" title="Apple A7">Apple A7</a> was also a highlight during the event:
</p>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1244412712">.mw-parser-output .templatequote{overflow:hidden;margin:1em 0;padding:0 32px}.mw-parser-output .templatequotecite{line-height:1.5em;text-align:left;margin-top:0}@media(min-width:500px){.mw-parser-output .templatequotecite{padding-left:1.6em}}</style><blockquote class="templatequote"><p>"This is the first-ever 64-bit processor in a phone of any kind. I don't think the other guys are even talking about it yet. Why go through all this? The benefits are huge. The A7 is up to twice as fast as the previous-generation system at CPU tasks, and up to twice as fast at graphics tasks, too."</p></blockquote><div class="templatequotecite"><p style="display: inline; padding-left: 2.3em;">—&#8202;<a href="/wiki/Phil_Schiller" title="Phil Schiller">Phil Schiller</a>, Apple keynote at <a href="/wiki/4_Infinite_Loop" class="mw-redirect" title="4 Infinite Loop">4 Infinite Loop</a> on September 10, 2013<sup id="cite&#95;ref-64bit&#95;121-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-64bit-121"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>121<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><span style="display:none" data-plural="1"></span></p></div>
<p>Schiller then showed demos of <i><a href="/wiki/Infinity_Blade_III" title="Infinity Blade III">Infinity Blade III</a></i> to demonstrate the A7's processing power and the iPhone&#160;5S camera using unretouched photographs.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-usatodayrecap&#95;122-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-usatodayrecap-122"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>122<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The release of <a href="/wiki/IOS_7" title="IOS 7">iOS 7</a> on September 18, 2013, was also announced during the keynote.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-usatodayrecap&#95;122-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-usatodayrecap-122"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>122<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(October_22,_2013)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28October_22.2C_2013.29"></span>Apple special event (October 22, 2013)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=94" title="Edit section: Apple special event (October 22, 2013)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Apple held a second Fall event in 2013 under the name of "We still have a lot to cover."<sup id="cite&#95;ref-123" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-123"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>123<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> This event saw the unveiling of the <a href="/wiki/IPad_Air" title="IPad Air">iPad Air</a>, the second-generation <a href="/wiki/IPad_mini_with_Retina_display" class="mw-redirect" title="IPad mini with Retina display">iPad mini with Retina display</a>, and updates to the <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Pro" title="MacBook Pro">MacBook Pro</a> line. Tim Cook also announced that <a href="/wiki/OS_X_Mavericks" title="OS X Mavericks">OS X Mavericks</a> would be available for free.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2014">2014</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=95" title="Edit section: 2014"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2014_(June_2–6,_2014)"><span id="WWDC_2014_.28June_2.E2.80.936.2C_2014.29"></span>WWDC 2014 (June 2–6, 2014)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=96" title="Edit section: WWDC 2014 (June 2–6, 2014)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>At Moscone West, Apple presented the new version of its operating systems: <a href="/wiki/OS_X_Yosemite" title="OS X Yosemite">OS X Yosemite</a> and <a href="/wiki/IOS_8" title="IOS 8">iOS 8</a>, both scheduled for release in the fall.
</p><p>As a <a href="/wiki/%22One_more_thing_...%22" class="mw-redirect" title="&quot;One more thing ...&quot;">“one more thing”</a> specifically for developers, Apple announced the new programming language <a href="/wiki/Swift_(programming_language)" title="Swift (programming language)">Swift</a>, meant to interoperate with and eventually replace <a href="/wiki/Objective-C" title="Objective-C">Objective-C</a> on all Apple platforms.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-124" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-124"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>124<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>This keynote was streamed live on June 2, 2014.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(September_9,_2014)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28September_9.2C_2014.29"></span>Apple special event (September 9, 2014)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=97" title="Edit section: Apple special event (September 9, 2014)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Presented on the "Wish we could say more" event was the most anticipated<sup id="cite&#95;ref-125" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-125"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>125<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> <a href="/wiki/IPhone_6" title="IPhone 6">iPhone 6 and 6 Plus</a> as well as the <a href="/wiki/Contactless_payment" title="Contactless payment">contactless payment</a> system <a href="/wiki/Apple_Pay" title="Apple Pay">Apple Pay</a>. The <a href="/wiki/Apple_Watch" title="Apple Watch">Apple Watch</a>, the company's first <a href="/wiki/Smartwatch" title="Smartwatch">smartwatch</a>, was introduced, months before its release in April 2015. The event took place at <a href="/wiki/Flint_Center" class="mw-redirect" title="Flint Center">Flint Center</a> in Cupertino.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(October_16,_2014)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28October_16.2C_2014.29"></span>Apple special event (October 16, 2014)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=98" title="Edit section: Apple special event (October 16, 2014)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Apple's "It's been way too long" media event took place on October 16, 2014.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-126" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-126"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>126<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The company used this event to unveil the <a href="/wiki/IPad_Air_2" title="IPad Air 2">iPad Air 2</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPad_mini_3" class="mw-redirect" title="IPad mini 3">iPad mini 3</a>, and an updated 27-inch model of the <a href="/wiki/IMac" title="IMac">iMac</a> featuring a 5K <a href="/wiki/Retina_Display" class="mw-redirect" title="Retina Display">Retina display</a>.
</p><p>This keynote was streamed live on October 16, 2014.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2015">2015</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=99" title="Edit section: 2015"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(March_9,_2015)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28March_9.2C_2015.29"></span>Apple special event (March 9, 2015)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=100" title="Edit section: Apple special event (March 9, 2015)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Apple Special Event 2015 had the tagline "Spring Forward" and was broadcast live from <a href="/wiki/Cupertino" class="mw-redirect" title="Cupertino">Cupertino</a> on <a href="/wiki/Apple_Inc." title="Apple Inc.">Apple</a>'s website on March 9, 2015. It announced the release date and pricing for the anticipated <a href="/wiki/Apple_Watch" title="Apple Watch">Apple Watch</a>, the <a href="/wiki/MacBook" title="MacBook">MacBook</a>'s fourth redesign and <a href="/wiki/IOS_8.2" class="mw-redirect" title="IOS 8.2">iOS 8.2</a>'s same day release.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-127" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-127"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>127<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>  Apple also announced ResearchKit, a library designed to enable researchers to make study applications where participants can download them on their phones, electronic enroll &amp; consent, and send survey and sensor data.  Five launch studies were introduced, including <a href="/wiki/Euan_Ashley" title="Euan Ashley">My Heart Counts</a>, which enrolled over 11,000 participants in a single day.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-128" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-128"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>128<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2015_(June_8–12,_2015)"><span id="WWDC_2015_.28June_8.E2.80.9312.2C_2015.29"></span>WWDC 2015 (June 8–12, 2015)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=101" title="Edit section: WWDC 2015 (June 8–12, 2015)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1305433154" /><table class="box-Unreferenced&#95;section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img alt="icon" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/60px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/120px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></span></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a href="/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events" title="Special:EditPage/List of Apple Inc. media events">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">April 2024</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>WWDC 2015 was held in Moscone Center West in San Francisco. The major announcements were the new features of <a href="/wiki/IOS_9" title="IOS 9">iOS 9</a>, the next version of OS X called <a href="/wiki/OS_X_El_Capitan" title="OS X El Capitan">OS X El Capitan</a>, the first major software update to the <a href="/wiki/Apple_Watch" title="Apple Watch">Apple Watch</a>, the June 30 debut of <a href="/wiki/Apple_Music" title="Apple Music">Apple Music</a>, and news that the programming language <a href="/wiki/Swift_(programming_language)" title="Swift (programming language)">Swift</a> was becoming <a href="/wiki/Open-source_software" title="Open-source software">open-source software</a> supporting <a href="/wiki/IOS" title="IOS">iOS</a>, <a href="/wiki/MacOS" title="MacOS">OS X</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Linux" title="Linux">Linux</a>. The Beer Bash was held at the Yerba Buena Gardens on June 11. <a href="/wiki/Walk_the_Moon" title="Walk the Moon">Walk the Moon</a> performed there.
</p><p>This keynote was streamed live on June 8, 2015.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(September_9,_2015)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28September_9.2C_2015.29"></span>Apple special event (September 9, 2015)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=102" title="Edit section: Apple special event (September 9, 2015)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>The "Hey Siri, give us a hint" event<sup id="cite&#95;ref-129" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-129"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>129<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-cnet-hey-siri-event-invitations&#95;130-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-cnet-hey-siri-event-invitations-130"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>130<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> was held at the 7,000-seat <a href="/wiki/Bill_Graham_Civic_Auditorium" title="Bill Graham Civic Auditorium">Bill Graham Civic Auditorium</a> in <a href="/wiki/San_Francisco" title="San Francisco">San Francisco</a>. Apple announced and previewed <a href="/wiki/WatchOS" title="WatchOS">watchOS</a> 2 with native apps; the long-anticipated <a href="/wiki/Apple_TV_(device)" title="Apple TV (device)">Apple TV</a> update - with <a href="/wiki/App_Store_(iOS)" class="mw-redirect" title="App Store (iOS)">App Store</a>, <a href="/wiki/Siri_Remote" title="Siri Remote">Siri Remote</a> and <a href="/wiki/TvOS" title="TvOS">tvOS</a>; <a href="/wiki/IPhone_6S" class="mw-redirect" title="IPhone 6S">iPhone 6S</a> and <a href="/wiki/IPhone_6S_Plus" class="mw-redirect" title="IPhone 6S Plus">iPhone 6S Plus</a> with the <a href="/wiki/Apple_A9" title="Apple A9">Apple A9</a>, <a href="/wiki/3D_Touch" class="mw-redirect" title="3D Touch">3D Touch</a>, 12MP camera; <a href="/wiki/IOS_9" title="IOS 9">iOS 9</a> update coming September 16;  and <a href="/wiki/IPad_Mini_4" title="IPad Mini 4">iPad Mini 4</a> together with <a href="/wiki/IPad_Pro" title="IPad Pro">iPad Pro</a> with 12.9" Retina display, optional keyboard/cover, and the <a href="/wiki/Apple_Pencil" title="Apple Pencil">Apple Pencil</a> stylus. <a href="/wiki/OneRepublic" title="OneRepublic">OneRepublic</a> performed at the event.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-cnet-hey-siri-event-summary&#95;131-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-cnet-hey-siri-event-summary-131"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>131<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>This keynote was streamed live on September 9, 2015. For the first time, <a href="/wiki/Microsoft_Windows" title="Microsoft Windows">Windows</a> users were able to watch it live using Microsoft Edge, the native <a href="/wiki/Windows_10" title="Windows 10">Windows 10</a> browser.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2016">2016</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=103" title="Edit section: 2016"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(March_21,_2016)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28March_21.2C_2016.29"></span>Apple special event (March 21, 2016)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=104" title="Edit section: Apple special event (March 21, 2016)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Apple invited the press media for its event "Let us loop you in" on March 21, 2016, in their own theatre "Town Hall" (at <a href="/wiki/Apple_Infinite_Loop" class="mw-redirect" title="Apple Infinite Loop">1 Infinite Loop</a>).<sup id="cite&#95;ref-132" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-132"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>132<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>  <a href="/wiki/IPad_Pro" title="IPad Pro">iPad Pro (9.7-inch)</a>, the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_SE_(1st_generation)" title="IPhone SE (1st generation)">first-generation iPhone SE</a>, CareKit and updates to <a href="/wiki/Apple_Watch" title="Apple Watch">Apple Watch</a>, <a href="/wiki/HealthKit" class="mw-redirect" title="HealthKit">HealthKit</a>, ResearchKit and <a href="/wiki/TvOS" title="TvOS">tvOS</a> were released.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2016_(June_13–17,_2016)"><span id="WWDC_2016_.28June_13.E2.80.9317.2C_2016.29"></span>WWDC 2016 (June 13–17, 2016)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=105" title="Edit section: WWDC 2016 (June 13–17, 2016)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1305433154" /><table class="box-Unreferenced&#95;section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img alt="icon" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/60px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/120px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></span></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a href="/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events" title="Special:EditPage/List of Apple Inc. media events">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">April 2024</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>Apple invited the press media and developers for its event on June 13, 2016, at <a href="/wiki/Moscone_West" class="mw-redirect" title="Moscone West">Moscone West</a> to unveil new versions of <a href="/wiki/IOS" title="IOS">iOS</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS" title="WatchOS">watchOS</a>, <a href="/wiki/TvOS" title="TvOS">tvOS</a>, and <a href="/wiki/MacOS" title="MacOS">macOS</a>, a revamped <a href="/wiki/Apple_Music" title="Apple Music">Apple Music</a> design, and the <a href="/wiki/Swift_Playgrounds" title="Swift Playgrounds">Swift Playgrounds</a> app - a learning tool for programming.
</p><p>This keynote was streamed live on June 13, 2016.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(September_7,_2016)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28September_7.2C_2016.29"></span>Apple special event (September 7, 2016)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=106" title="Edit section: Apple special event (September 7, 2016)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Apple hosted a media event on September 7, 2016, with the invitation's tagline "See you on the 7th".<sup id="cite&#95;ref-sept2016invite&#95;133-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-sept2016invite-133"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>133<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> <a href="/wiki/IPhone_7" title="IPhone 7">iPhone 7 and 7 Plus</a> were announced at the event along with Apple's new wireless <a href="/wiki/AirPods" title="AirPods">AirPods</a>. <a href="/wiki/IOS_10" title="IOS 10">iOS 10</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS_3" class="mw-redirect" title="WatchOS 3">watchOS 3</a> and <a href="/wiki/TvOS_10" class="mw-redirect" title="TvOS 10">tvOS 10</a> were released six days later. <a href="/wiki/Sia" title="Sia">Sia</a> performed at the event.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-134" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-134"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>134<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(October_27,_2016)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28October_27.2C_2016.29"></span>Apple special event (October 27, 2016)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=107" title="Edit section: Apple special event (October 27, 2016)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Apple hosted another media event on October 27, 2016, with the tagline "hello again".<sup id="cite&#95;ref-oct2016invite&#95;135-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-oct2016invite-135"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>135<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> A new generation of <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Pro" title="MacBook Pro">MacBook Pro</a> and a new <a href="/wiki/Apple_TV_(software)" class="mw-redirect" title="Apple TV (software)">TV app</a> was announced at the event.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2017">2017</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=108" title="Edit section: 2017"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2017_(June_5–9,_2017)"><span id="WWDC_2017_.28June_5.E2.80.939.2C_2017.29"></span>WWDC 2017 (June 5–9, 2017)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=109" title="Edit section: WWDC 2017 (June 5–9, 2017)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1305433154" /><table class="box-Unreferenced&#95;section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img alt="icon" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/60px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/120px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></span></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a href="/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events" title="Special:EditPage/List of Apple Inc. media events">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">April 2024</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>WWDC 2017 was held in <a href="/wiki/San_Jose,_California" title="San Jose, California">San Jose, California</a>, at its <a href="/wiki/San_Jose_Convention_Center" title="San Jose Convention Center">Convention Center</a>. There were multiple hardware announcements at the event. Software announcements included <a href="/wiki/IOS_11" title="IOS 11">iOS 11</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS_4" class="mw-redirect" title="WatchOS 4">watchOS 4</a>, <a href="/wiki/TvOS_11" class="mw-redirect" title="TvOS 11">tvOS 11</a> and <a href="/wiki/MacOS_High_Sierra" title="MacOS High Sierra">macOS High Sierra</a>. An all-new <a href="/wiki/IPad_Pro#Second_generation" title="IPad Pro">second generation iPad Pro</a> model was introduced with thinner bezels and a 10.5-inch screen size. It acquired many of the specs from the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_7" title="IPhone 7">iPhone 7</a> and an <a href="/wiki/Apple_A10X" title="Apple A10X">A10X</a> chip. The iPad Pro 12.9-inch was also refreshed with updated internals. Apple previewed several new Mac models, such as the <a href="/wiki/MacBook" title="MacBook">MacBook</a>, <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Pro" title="MacBook Pro">MacBook Pro</a>, <a href="/wiki/IMac" title="IMac">iMac</a>, and an all-new <a href="/wiki/IMac_Pro" title="IMac Pro">iMac Pro</a> and all-new <a href="/wiki/HomePod" title="HomePod">HomePod</a>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(September_12,_2017)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28September_12.2C_2017.29"></span>Apple special event (September 12, 2017)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=110" title="Edit section: Apple special event (September 12, 2017)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Apple hosted a media event on September 12, 2017, with the tagline "Let's meet at our place". The tagline was a reference to Apple holding its first-ever event at the newly completed <a href="/wiki/Steve_Jobs_Theater" title="Steve Jobs Theater">Steve Jobs Theater</a> in the <a href="/wiki/Apple_Park" title="Apple Park">Apple Park</a> campus.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-136" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-136"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>136<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> At the event, <a href="/wiki/Apple_Watch_Series_3" class="mw-redirect" title="Apple Watch Series 3">Apple Watch Series 3</a>, <a href="/wiki/Apple_TV_4K" class="mw-redirect" title="Apple TV 4K">Apple TV 4K</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPhone_8" title="IPhone 8">iPhone 8 and 8 Plus</a>, and <a href="/wiki/IPhone_X" title="IPhone X">iPhone X</a> were announced at the event. <a href="/wiki/IOS_11" title="IOS 11">iOS 11</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS" title="WatchOS">watchOS</a> 4 and <a href="/wiki/TvOS" title="TvOS">tvOS</a> 11 were released a week later.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2018">2018</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=111" title="Edit section: 2018"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(March_27,_2018)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28March_27.2C_2018.29"></span>Apple special event (March 27, 2018)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=112" title="Edit section: Apple special event (March 27, 2018)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Apple hosted a media event on March 27, 2018, with the tagline "Let's take a field trip". It was held at the <a href="/wiki/Lane_Technical_College_Prep_High_School" class="mw-redirect" title="Lane Technical College Prep High School">Lane Technical College Prep High School</a> in <a href="/wiki/Chicago" title="Chicago">Chicago</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-137" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-137"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>137<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The <a href="/wiki/IPad_(6th_generation)" title="IPad (6th generation)">iPad (6th generation)</a> was announced at the education-focused event.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-138" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-138"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>138<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2018_(June_4–8,_2018)"><span id="WWDC_2018_.28June_4.E2.80.938.2C_2018.29"></span>WWDC 2018 (June 4–8, 2018)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=113" title="Edit section: WWDC 2018 (June 4–8, 2018)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>WWDC 2018 was held at the <a href="/wiki/San_Jose_Convention_Center" title="San Jose Convention Center">San Jose Convention Center</a> in <a href="/wiki/California" title="California">California</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-139" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-139"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>139<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The announcements at the event included <a href="/wiki/IOS_12" title="IOS 12">iOS 12</a>, <a href="/wiki/MacOS_Mojave" title="MacOS Mojave">macOS Mojave</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS#watchOS_5.x" title="WatchOS">watchOS 5</a>, and <a href="/wiki/TvOS_12" class="mw-redirect" title="TvOS 12">tvOS 12</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-140" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-140"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>140<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Panic!_at_the_Disco" title="Panic! at the Disco">Panic! at the Disco</a> performed at the Bash at Discovery Meadow Park.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(September_12,_2018)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28September_12.2C_2018.29"></span>Apple special event (September 12, 2018)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=114" title="Edit section: Apple special event (September 12, 2018)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1305433154" /><table class="box-Unreferenced&#95;section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img alt="icon" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/60px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/120px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></span></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a href="/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events" title="Special:EditPage/List of Apple Inc. media events">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">April 2024</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
