<p>Apple hosted another media event on September 12, 2018, with the tagline "Gather round". It was held at the <a href="/wiki/Steve_Jobs_Theater" title="Steve Jobs Theater">Steve Jobs Theater</a> in the <a href="/wiki/Apple_Park" title="Apple Park">Apple Park</a> campus. The <a href="/wiki/Apple_Watch#Fourth_generation_(Series_4)" title="Apple Watch">Apple Watch Series 4</a>, the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_XS" title="IPhone XS">iPhone XS and XS Max</a>, and the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_XR" title="IPhone XR">iPhone XR</a> were announced at the event. <a href="/wiki/IOS_12" title="IOS 12">iOS 12</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS" title="WatchOS">watchOS</a> 5 and <a href="/wiki/TvOS" title="TvOS">tvOS</a> 12 were released five days later.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(October_30,_2018)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28October_30.2C_2018.29"></span>Apple special event (October 30, 2018)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=115" title="Edit section: Apple special event (October 30, 2018)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Apple hosted another media event on October 30, 2018, with the tagline "There's more in the making". It was held at the <a href="/wiki/Brooklyn_Academy_of_Music" title="Brooklyn Academy of Music">Brooklyn Academy of Music</a> in <a href="/wiki/Brooklyn" title="Brooklyn">Brooklyn</a>, <a href="/wiki/New_York_City" title="New York City">New York City</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-141" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-141"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>141<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The new <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Air" title="MacBook Air">MacBook Air</a> 2018 model, <a href="/wiki/Mac_Mini" title="Mac Mini">Mac Mini</a> 2018 model, and the 11-inch and 12.9-inch <a href="/wiki/IPad_Pro" title="IPad Pro">third generation iPad Pro</a> were announced at the event.
</p><p>The event closed with a live performance of <a href="/wiki/Lana_Del_Rey" title="Lana Del Rey">Lana Del Rey</a> singing <i><a href="/wiki/How_to_Disappear" class="mw-redirect" title="How to Disappear">How to Disappear</a></i> and an edited version of <i><a href="/wiki/Venice_Bitch" title="Venice Bitch">Venice Bitch</a></i> from her upcoming album <i><a href="/wiki/Norman_Fucking_Rockwell!" title="Norman Fucking Rockwell!">Norman Fucking Rockwell!</a></i><sup id="cite&#95;ref-142" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-142"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>142<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2019">2019</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=116" title="Edit section: 2019"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(March_25,_2019)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28March_25.2C_2019.29"></span>Apple special event (March 25, 2019)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=117" title="Edit section: Apple special event (March 25, 2019)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Apple hosted a media event on March 25, 2019, with the tagline "It's show time". It was held at the <a href="/wiki/Steve_Jobs_Theater" title="Steve Jobs Theater">Steve Jobs Theater</a> in the <a href="/wiki/Apple_Park" title="Apple Park">Apple Park</a> campus.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-143" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-143"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>143<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-144" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-144"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>144<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Apple_News%2B" class="mw-redirect" title="Apple News+">Apple News+</a>, <a href="/wiki/Apple_Card" title="Apple Card">Apple Card</a>, <a href="/wiki/Apple_Arcade" title="Apple Arcade">Apple Arcade</a> and <a href="/wiki/Apple_TV%2B" class="mw-redirect" title="Apple TV+">Apple TV+</a> were announced at this event.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2019_(June_3–7,_2019)"><span id="WWDC_2019_.28June_3.E2.80.937.2C_2019.29"></span>WWDC 2019 (June 3–7, 2019)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=118" title="Edit section: WWDC 2019 (June 3–7, 2019)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>WWDC 2019 was held at the San Jose Convention Center in California.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-145" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-145"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>145<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-146" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-146"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>146<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> At the keynote that kicked off the event, Apple announced <a href="/wiki/IOS_13" title="IOS 13">iOS 13</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPadOS" title="IPadOS">iPadOS</a>, <a href="/wiki/MacOS_Catalina" title="MacOS Catalina">macOS Catalina</a>, <a href="/wiki/TvOS_13" class="mw-redirect" title="TvOS 13">tvOS 13</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS_6" class="mw-redirect" title="WatchOS 6">watchOS 6</a>, a redesigned <a href="/wiki/Mac_Pro" title="Mac Pro">Mac Pro</a>, the <a href="/wiki/Pro_Display_XDR" title="Pro Display XDR">Pro Display XDR</a>,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-147" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-147"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>147<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> and the new <a href="/wiki/Swift_(programming_language)" title="Swift (programming language)">SwiftUI</a> framework.
</p><p>WWDC19 hosted over 5,500 attendees, including 350 winners of Apple's student challenge. For the latter, a separate "Scholarship Kickoff" event was organized on June 2, one day before the conference start.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-148" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-148"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>148<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p><a href="/wiki/Weezer" title="Weezer">Weezer</a> performed at the "Bash" meetup organized as part of WWDC at Discovery Meadow (located about half a kilometer from the convention center).<sup id="cite&#95;ref-149" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-149"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>149<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_special_event_(September_10,_2019)"><span id="Apple_special_event_.28September_10.2C_2019.29"></span>Apple special event (September 10, 2019)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=119" title="Edit section: Apple special event (September 10, 2019)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Apple hosted a special event on September 10, 2019, with the tagline “By innovation only.” It was held at the <a href="/wiki/Steve_Jobs_Theater" title="Steve Jobs Theater">Steve Jobs Theater</a> in the <a href="/wiki/Apple_Park" title="Apple Park">Apple Park</a> campus. <a href="/wiki/Apple_TV%2B" class="mw-redirect" title="Apple TV+">Apple TV+</a> updates, <a href="/wiki/Apple_Watch#Fifth_generation_(Series_5_&amp;_SE_(1st_gen))" title="Apple Watch">Apple Watch Series 5</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPhone_11" title="IPhone 11">iPhone 11</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPhone_11_Pro" title="IPhone 11 Pro">iPhone 11 Pro and 11 Pro Max</a>, and the <a href="/wiki/IPad_(7th_generation)" title="IPad (7th generation)">iPad (7th generation)</a> were announced at the event. <a href="/wiki/IOS_13" title="IOS 13">iOS 13</a> and <a href="/wiki/WatchOS" title="WatchOS">watchOS</a> 6 were released nine days later. <a href="/wiki/IPadOS" title="IPadOS">iPadOS</a> and <a href="/wiki/TvOS" title="TvOS">tvOS</a> 13 were also released the following five days.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-150" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-150"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>150<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>The event was streamed live on <a href="/wiki/YouTube" title="YouTube">YouTube</a> for the first time, in addition to the usual streaming on Apple's website and through an <a href="/wiki/Apple_TV_(device)" title="Apple TV (device)">Apple TV</a> channel.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2020">2020</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=120" title="Edit section: 2020"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2020_(June_22–26,_2020)"><span id="WWDC_2020_.28June_22.E2.80.9326.2C_2020.29"></span>WWDC 2020 (June 22–26, 2020)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=121" title="Edit section: WWDC 2020 (June 22–26, 2020)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Due to the <a href="/wiki/COVID-19_pandemic_in_the_United_States" title="COVID-19 pandemic in the United States">COVID-19 pandemic</a>, WWDC 2020 took place as a virtual conference for the first time, and the headline keynote event was pre-recorded (also for the first time).<sup id="cite&#95;ref-2020&#95;online&#95;151-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-2020_online-151"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>151<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The announcements on June 22 included <a href="/wiki/IOS_14" title="IOS 14">iOS 14</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPadOS_14" title="IPadOS 14">iPadOS 14</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS" title="WatchOS">watchOS 7</a>, <a href="/wiki/TvOS" title="TvOS">tvOS 14</a>, <a href="/wiki/MacOS_Big_Sur" title="MacOS Big Sur">macOS Big Sur</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Mac_transition_to_Apple_silicon" title="Mac transition to Apple silicon">Mac transition to Apple silicon</a>. The keynote was recorded at <a href="/wiki/Apple_Park" title="Apple Park">Apple Park</a> in <a href="/wiki/Cupertino,_California" title="Cupertino, California">Cupertino, California</a>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_event_(September_15,_2020)"><span id="Apple_event_.28September_15.2C_2020.29"></span>Apple event (September 15, 2020)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=122" title="Edit section: Apple event (September 15, 2020)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>An Apple Event was held on September 15, 2020, with the tagline "Time flies."<sup id="cite&#95;ref-152" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-152"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>152<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> It took place as a virtual event, pre-recorded at <a href="/wiki/Apple_Park" title="Apple Park">Apple Park</a>. Amongst the many announcements include the <a href="/wiki/Apple_Watch_Series_6" class="mw-redirect" title="Apple Watch Series 6">Apple Watch Series 6</a>, the <a href="/wiki/Apple_Watch_SE" class="mw-redirect" title="Apple Watch SE">Apple Watch SE</a>, the fourth-generation <a href="/wiki/IPad_Air_(2020)" class="mw-redirect" title="IPad Air (2020)">iPad Air</a>, and the <a href="/wiki/IPad_(8th_generation)" title="IPad (8th generation)">iPad (8th generation)</a>. <a href="/wiki/IOS_14" title="IOS 14">iOS 14</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPadOS_14" title="IPadOS 14">iPadOS 14</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS" title="WatchOS">watchOS</a> 7 and <a href="/wiki/TvOS" title="TvOS">tvOS</a> 14 were released a day later.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-153" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-153"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>153<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_event_(October_13,_2020)"><span id="Apple_event_.28October_13.2C_2020.29"></span>Apple event (October 13, 2020)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=123" title="Edit section: Apple event (October 13, 2020)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>An Apple Event was held on October 13, 2020, with the tagline "Hi, Speed" referring to the support of the <a href="/wiki/5G" title="5G">5G</a> cellular standard. It also took place as a virtual event at <a href="/wiki/Apple_Park" title="Apple Park">Apple Park</a>, just like the previous one held on September 15. The <a href="/wiki/HomePod_mini" class="mw-redirect" title="HomePod mini">HomePod mini</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPhone_12" title="IPhone 12">iPhone 12 mini</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPhone_12" title="IPhone 12">iPhone 12</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPhone_12_Pro" title="IPhone 12 Pro">iPhone 12 Pro</a>, and <a href="/wiki/IPhone_12_Pro" title="IPhone 12 Pro">iPhone 12 Pro Max</a> were announced at the event. Preorders for iPhone 12 and iPhone 12 Pro began on October 16 and shipping began on October 23. The iPhone 12 mini and iPhone 12 Pro Max were available for preorder on November 6, and started shipping on November 13. <a href="/wiki/Verizon_Communications" class="mw-redirect" title="Verizon Communications">Verizon</a> CEO <a href="/wiki/Hans_Vestberg" title="Hans Vestberg">Hans Vestberg</a> appeared alongside <a href="/wiki/Tim_Cook" title="Tim Cook">Tim Cook</a> to discuss <a href="/wiki/5G" title="5G">5G</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-Kruppa&#95;20&#95;154-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Kruppa_20-154"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>154<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-Blumenthal&#95;20&#95;155-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Blumenthal_20-155"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>155<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-Higgins&#95;20&#95;156-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Higgins_20-156"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>156<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_event_(November_10,_2020)"><span id="Apple_event_.28November_10.2C_2020.29"></span>Apple event (November 10, 2020)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=124" title="Edit section: Apple event (November 10, 2020)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>An Apple Event was held on November 10, 2020, with the tagline "One more thing." It also took place as a virtual event at <a href="/wiki/Apple_Park" title="Apple Park">Apple Park</a>, just like the previous two held on September 15 and October 13. It focused on the <a href="/wiki/Apple_M1" title="Apple M1">Apple M1</a>, the new Apple silicon chip,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-157" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-157"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>157<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> and the new <a href="/wiki/Apple_silicon" title="Apple silicon">Apple silicon</a>-powered models of the <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Air_(Apple_silicon)" title="MacBook Air (Apple silicon)">MacBook Air</a>,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-158" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-158"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>158<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Mac_Mini" title="Mac Mini">Mac mini</a>,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-159" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-159"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>159<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> and 13" <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Pro" title="MacBook Pro">MacBook Pro</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-160" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-160"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>160<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> <a href="/wiki/MacOS_Big_Sur" title="MacOS Big Sur">macOS Big Sur</a> was released two days later.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-161" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-161"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>161<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> <a href="/wiki/John_Hodgman" title="John Hodgman">John Hodgman</a> appeared in a cameo at the end of the event.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2021">2021</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=125" title="Edit section: 2021"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_event_(April_20,_2021)"><span id="Apple_event_.28April_20.2C_2021.29"></span>Apple event (April 20, 2021)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=126" title="Edit section: Apple event (April 20, 2021)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1305433154" /><table class="box-Unreferenced&#95;section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img alt="icon" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/60px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/120px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></span></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a href="/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events" title="Special:EditPage/List of Apple Inc. media events">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">April 2024</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>An Apple Event was held on April 20, 2021, with the tagline "Spring loaded." It took place as a <a href="/wiki/Virtual_event" title="Virtual event">virtual event</a> at <a href="/wiki/Apple_Park" title="Apple Park">Apple Park</a>. It revealed a new <a href="/wiki/IPad_Pro_(5th_generation)" title="IPad Pro (5th generation)">iPad Pro</a> and <a href="/wiki/IMac" title="IMac">iMac</a> with the <a href="/wiki/Apple_M1" title="Apple M1">Apple M1</a> chip, <a href="/wiki/Apple_TV_4K" class="mw-redirect" title="Apple TV 4K">Apple TV 4K</a>, <a href="/wiki/AirTag_(tracker)" class="mw-redirect" title="AirTag (tracker)">AirTag</a>, and a new purple color option for <a href="/wiki/IPhone_12" title="IPhone 12">iPhone 12</a> and <a href="/wiki/IPhone_12" title="IPhone 12">12 Mini</a>, and announced new features for <a href="/wiki/IOS_14" title="IOS 14">iOS 14</a>.7.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2021_(June_7–11,_2021)"><span id="WWDC_2021_.28June_7.E2.80.9311.2C_2021.29"></span>WWDC 2021 (June 7–11, 2021)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=127" title="Edit section: WWDC 2021 (June 7–11, 2021)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>As with the previous WWDC in 2020, WWDC 2021 took place as a virtual event, with its keynote pre-recorded at <a href="/wiki/Apple_Park" title="Apple Park">Apple Park</a>. The announcements at the online special event keynote on June 7 included <a href="/wiki/IOS_15" title="IOS 15">iOS 15</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPadOS_15" title="IPadOS 15">iPadOS 15</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS" title="WatchOS">watchOS 8</a>, <a href="/wiki/TvOS" title="TvOS">tvOS 15</a> and <a href="/wiki/MacOS_Monterey" title="MacOS Monterey">macOS Monterey</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-162" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-162"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>162<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_event_(September_14,_2021)"><span id="Apple_event_.28September_14.2C_2021.29"></span>Apple event (September 14, 2021)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=128" title="Edit section: Apple event (September 14, 2021)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>An Apple Event was held on September 14, 2021, with the tagline "California streaming." It also took place as a virtual event at <a href="/wiki/Apple_Park" title="Apple Park">Apple Park</a> and at locations across California. During the event, it revealed the <a href="/wiki/IPad_(9th_generation)" title="IPad (9th generation)">iPad (9th generation)</a> with the <a href="/wiki/Apple_A13" title="Apple A13">Apple A13</a> chip, the new <a href="/wiki/IPad_Mini_(6th_generation)" title="IPad Mini (6th generation)">iPad mini</a> with the <a href="/wiki/Apple_A15" title="Apple A15">Apple A15</a> chip, the <a href="/wiki/Apple_Watch" title="Apple Watch">Apple Watch Series 7</a>, and the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_13" title="IPhone 13">iPhone 13 mini</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPhone_13" title="IPhone 13">iPhone 13</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPhone_13_Pro" title="IPhone 13 Pro">iPhone 13 Pro</a>, and <a href="/wiki/IPhone_13_Pro_Max" class="mw-redirect" title="IPhone 13 Pro Max">iPhone 13 Pro Max</a>. <a href="/wiki/IOS_15" title="IOS 15">iOS 15</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPadOS_15" title="IPadOS 15">iPadOS 15</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS" title="WatchOS">watchOS 8</a>, and <a href="/wiki/TvOS" title="TvOS">tvOS 15</a> were released six days later.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-163" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-163"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>163<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Pre-orders for iPhone 13, iPhone 13 mini, iPhone 13 Pro, and iPhone 13 Pro Max began on September 17 and shipping began on September 24.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_event_(October_18,_2021)"><span id="Apple_event_.28October_18.2C_2021.29"></span>Apple event (October 18, 2021)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=129" title="Edit section: Apple event (October 18, 2021)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>An Apple Event was held on October 18, 2021, with the tagline "Unleashed." It again took place as a virtual event and was livestreamed from <a href="/wiki/Apple_Park" title="Apple Park">Apple Park</a>. During the event, Apple announced an <a href="/wiki/Apple_Music" title="Apple Music">Apple Music</a> Voice Plan, new color options for <a href="/wiki/HomePod_Mini" title="HomePod Mini">HomePod mini</a> (blue, yellow, and orange, which became available in November), <a href="/wiki/AirPods" title="AirPods">AirPods (3rd generation)</a>, and new <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Pro" title="MacBook Pro">MacBook Pro 14" and 16"</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-:0&#95;164-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-:0-164"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>164<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-:1&#95;165-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-:1-165"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>165<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> <a href="/wiki/MacOS_Monterey" title="MacOS Monterey">MacOS Monterey</a> was released a week later.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-166" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-166"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>166<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The new MacBook models have a new design, bringing back previously removed ports, and are powered by Apple's <a href="/wiki/Apple_M1" title="Apple M1">M1 Pro</a> and <a href="/wiki/Apple_M1" title="Apple M1">M1 Max</a> processors, which replaced the <a href="/wiki/Intel" title="Intel">Intel</a> processors in the previous 16" model.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-:0&#95;164-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-:0-164"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>164<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-:1&#95;165-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-:1-165"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>165<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2022">2022</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=130" title="Edit section: 2022"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_event_(March_8,_2022)"><span id="Apple_event_.28March_8.2C_2022.29"></span>Apple event (March 8, 2022)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=131" title="Edit section: Apple event (March 8, 2022)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>An Apple Event was held on March 8, 2022, with the tagline "Peek performance." It was once again held as a virtual event. A new <a href="/wiki/IPhone_SE_(3rd_generation)" title="IPhone SE (3rd generation)">iPhone SE</a> with <a href="/wiki/5G" title="5G">5G</a> capabilities and an enhanced camera was announced, as well as new "Green/Alpine Green" color options for the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_13" title="IPhone 13">iPhone 13</a> and <a href="/wiki/IPhone_13_Pro" title="IPhone 13 Pro">iPhone 13 Pro</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-167" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-167"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>167<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> A new <a href="/wiki/IPad_Air_(5th_generation)" title="IPad Air (5th generation)">iPad Air</a> with the <a href="/wiki/Apple_M1" title="Apple M1">M1</a> chip and 5G was also announced at the event.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-168" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-168"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>168<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> A new desktop computer, the <a href="/wiki/Mac_Studio" title="Mac Studio">Mac Studio</a>, was also announced, along with the <a href="/wiki/M1_Ultra" class="mw-redirect" title="M1 Ultra">M1 Ultra</a> chip and the new <a href="/wiki/Apple_Studio_Display" title="Apple Studio Display">Apple Studio Display</a>, Apple's first consumer-grade monitor since the discontinuation of the <a href="/wiki/Apple_Thunderbolt_Display" title="Apple Thunderbolt Display">Apple Thunderbolt Display</a> in 2016.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-169" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-169"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>169<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-170" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-170"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>170<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-171" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-171"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>171<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2022_(June_6–10,_2022)"><span id="WWDC_2022_.28June_6.E2.80.9310.2C_2022.29"></span>WWDC 2022 (June 6–10, 2022)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=132" title="Edit section: WWDC 2022 (June 6–10, 2022)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>WWDC 2022 took place as a virtual event, although there was a special day at Apple Park on June 6, allowing developers and students to watch the online events together.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-172" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-172"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>172<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Software announcements at the online special event keynote on June 6 included <a href="/wiki/IOS_16" title="IOS 16">iOS 16</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPadOS_16" title="IPadOS 16">iPadOS 16</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS" title="WatchOS">watchOS 9</a>, <a href="/wiki/TvOS" title="TvOS">tvOS 16</a> and <a href="/wiki/MacOS_Ventura" title="MacOS Ventura">macOS Ventura</a>. Hardware announcements included the <a href="/wiki/Apple_M2" title="Apple M2">M2</a> chip and two Macs based on it, a redesigned <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Air_(Apple_silicon)" title="MacBook Air (Apple silicon)">MacBook Air</a> and an updated 13-inch <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Pro_(Apple_silicon)" title="MacBook Pro (Apple silicon)">MacBook Pro</a>.
</p><p>After two years of online only events due to the <a href="/wiki/COVID-19_pandemic" title="COVID-19 pandemic">COVID-19 pandemic</a>, WWDC 2022 was the first in-person Apple Event since WWDC 2019.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-173" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-173"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>173<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The opening keynote was still pre-recorded, with a limited number of developers and members of the press invited to <a href="/wiki/Apple_Park" title="Apple Park">Apple Park</a>. The viewing occurred at Caffè Macs inside Apple Park, with seating extending outside.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-174" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-174"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>174<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_event_(September_7,_2022)"><span id="Apple_event_.28September_7.2C_2022.29"></span>Apple event (September 7, 2022)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=133" title="Edit section: Apple event (September 7, 2022)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>An Apple Event was held on September 7, 2022, with the tagline "Far out." It took place as an in-person event<sup id="cite&#95;ref-175" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-175"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>175<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> at the <a href="/wiki/Steve_Jobs_Theater" title="Steve Jobs Theater">Steve Jobs Theater</a> at 10 a.m. <a href="/wiki/Pacific_Time_Zone" title="Pacific Time Zone">PDT</a>, making it the first event conducted in-person since the September 10, 2019 event.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-176" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-176"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>176<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> It also took place as a virtual event at <a href="/wiki/Apple_Park" title="Apple Park">Apple Park</a> and at locations across California, just like the previous one held on March 8. Apple announced the <a href="/wiki/Apple_Watch#Eighth_generation_(Series_8,_SE_(2nd_gen)_&amp;_Ultra)" title="Apple Watch">Apple Watch Series 8</a>, the second-generation <a href="/wiki/Apple_Watch#Eighth_generation_(Series_8,_SE_(2nd_gen)_&amp;_Ultra)" title="Apple Watch">Apple Watch SE</a>, the new <a href="/wiki/Apple_Watch#Eighth_generation_(Series_8,_SE_(2nd_gen)_&amp;_Ultra)" title="Apple Watch">Apple Watch Ultra</a>, the second-generation <a href="/wiki/AirPods_Pro" title="AirPods Pro">AirPods Pro</a>, the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_14" title="IPhone 14">iPhone 14</a> and <a href="/wiki/IPhone_14_Plus" class="mw-redirect" title="IPhone 14 Plus">iPhone 14 Plus</a> with the <a href="/wiki/Apple_A15" title="Apple A15">Apple A15</a> chip, and the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_14_Pro" title="IPhone 14 Pro">iPhone 14 Pro</a> and <a href="/wiki/IPhone_14_Pro_Max" class="mw-redirect" title="IPhone 14 Pro Max">iPhone 14 Pro Max</a> with the <a href="/wiki/Apple_A16" title="Apple A16">Apple A16</a> chip.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2023">2023</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=134" title="Edit section: 2023"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2023_(June_5–9,_2023)"><span id="WWDC_2023_.28June_5.E2.80.939.2C_2023.29"></span>WWDC 2023 (June 5–9, 2023)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=135" title="Edit section: WWDC 2023 (June 5–9, 2023)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>WWDC 2023 took place online with an in-person experience at Apple Park. Hardware announcements at the online special event keynote on June 5 included the 15-inch <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Air" title="MacBook Air">MacBook Air</a>, the next-generation Mac Studio with dual chips <a href="/wiki/M2_Max" class="mw-redirect" title="M2 Max">M2 Max</a> and <a href="/wiki/M2_Ultra" class="mw-redirect" title="M2 Ultra">M2 Ultra</a>, a newly upgraded <a href="/wiki/Mac_Pro" title="Mac Pro">Mac Pro</a> with an <a href="/wiki/M2_Ultra" class="mw-redirect" title="M2 Ultra">M2 Ultra</a> chip, and <a href="/wiki/Apple_Vision_Pro" title="Apple Vision Pro">Apple Vision Pro</a>, the new <a href="/wiki/Augmented_reality" title="Augmented reality">augmented reality</a> headset which also has dual chips <a href="/wiki/Apple_M2" title="Apple M2">Apple M2</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Apple_R1" class="mw-redirect" title="Apple R1">Apple R1</a>. Software announcements also included <a href="/wiki/IOS_17" title="IOS 17">iOS 17</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPadOS_17" title="IPadOS 17">iPadOS 17</a>, <a href="/wiki/MacOS_Sonoma" title="MacOS Sonoma">macOS Sonoma</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS_10" class="mw-redirect" title="WatchOS 10">watchOS 10</a>, <a href="/wiki/TvOS_17" class="mw-redirect" title="TvOS 17">tvOS 17</a>, and the <a href="/wiki/VisionOS" title="VisionOS">visionOS</a> operating system for the Apple Vision Pro.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-177" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-177"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>177<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-178" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-178"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>178<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_event_(September_12,_2023)"><span id="Apple_event_.28September_12.2C_2023.29"></span>Apple event (September 12, 2023)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=136" title="Edit section: Apple event (September 12, 2023)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>An Apple event was held on September 12, 2023, with the tagline "Wonderlust." It took place online with an in-person experience at Apple Park on September 12, 2023, at 10 a.m. <a href="/wiki/Pacific_Time_Zone" title="Pacific Time Zone">PT</a>. Apple announced Apple Watch Series 9, Apple Watch Series Ultra 2, <a href="/wiki/IPhone_15" title="IPhone 15">iPhone 15</a>, iPhone 15 Plus, <a href="/wiki/IPhone_15_Pro" title="IPhone 15 Pro">iPhone 15 Pro</a>, and iPhone 15 Pro Max. The entire iPhone 15 lineup switched to <a href="/wiki/USB-C" title="USB-C">USB-C</a>, replacing the  <a href="/wiki/Lightning_(connector)" title="Lightning (connector)">Lightning</a> port (originally introduced in 2012) which started off with iPhone 5 and ended with the iPhone 14 series making them the last iPhones to use the Lightning port, being said in October 2022 to comply with the new EU regulations. <a href="/wiki/IOS_17" title="IOS 17">iOS 17</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPadOS_17" title="IPadOS 17">iPadOS 17</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS_10" class="mw-redirect" title="WatchOS 10">watchOS 10</a> and <a href="/wiki/TvOS_17" class="mw-redirect" title="TvOS 17">tvOS 17</a> were released six days later, on September 18.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-179" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-179"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>179<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_event_(October_30,_2023)"><span id="Apple_event_.28October_30.2C_2023.29"></span>Apple event (October 30, 2023)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=137" title="Edit section: Apple event (October 30, 2023)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>An Apple event was held on October 30, 2023, with the tagline "Scary fast.", at 5 p.m. <a href="/wiki/Pacific_Time_Zone" title="Pacific Time Zone">Pacific Standard Time</a>, making it Apple's first nighttime event.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-180" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-180"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>180<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-181" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-181"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>181<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> It introduced MacBook Pro models and an iMac with <a href="/wiki/Apple_M3" title="Apple M3">Apple M3</a> family chips.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-182" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-182"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>182<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-183" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-183"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>183<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> It was Apple's first digital event to be shot on <a href="/wiki/IPhone" title="IPhone">iPhone</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-184" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-184"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>184<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2024">2024</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=138" title="Edit section: 2024"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_event_(May_7,_2024)"><span id="Apple_event_.28May_7.2C_2024.29"></span>Apple event (May 7, 2024)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=139" title="Edit section: Apple event (May 7, 2024)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>An Apple event was held on May 7, 2024 at 7:00 am <a href="/wiki/Pacific_Standard_Time" class="mw-redirect" title="Pacific Standard Time">Pacific Standard Time</a> with the tagline "Let loose." The event focused mainly on <a href="/wiki/IPad" title="IPad">iPads</a>, including the introduction of the <a href="/wiki/IPad_Pro_(7th_generation)" class="mw-redirect" title="IPad Pro (7th generation)">iPad Pro (7th generation)</a> with the new <a href="/wiki/Apple_M4" title="Apple M4">M4</a> chip, the <a href="/wiki/IPad_Air_(6th_generation)" class="mw-redirect" title="IPad Air (6th generation)">iPad Air (6th generation)</a>, <a href="/wiki/Magic_Keyboard_for_iPad" class="mw-redirect" title="Magic Keyboard for iPad">Magic Keyboard 2</a> and <a href="/wiki/Apple_Pencil_Pro" class="mw-redirect" title="Apple Pencil Pro">Apple Pencil Pro</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-185" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-185"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>185<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-186" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-186"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>186<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2024_(June_10–14,_2024)"><span id="WWDC_2024_.28June_10.E2.80.9314.2C_2024.29"></span>WWDC 2024 (June 10–14, 2024)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=140" title="Edit section: WWDC 2024 (June 10–14, 2024)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>WWDC 2024 took place online with an in-person experience at Apple Park.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-187" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-187"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>187<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Apple announced <a href="/wiki/IOS_18" title="IOS 18">iOS 18</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPadOS_18" title="IPadOS 18">iPadOS 18</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS_11" class="mw-redirect" title="WatchOS 11">watchOS 11</a>, <a href="/wiki/TvOS_18" class="mw-redirect" title="TvOS 18">tvOS 18</a>, <a href="/wiki/VisionOS_2" class="mw-redirect" title="VisionOS 2">visionOS 2</a>, and <a href="/wiki/MacOS_Sequoia" title="MacOS Sequoia">macOS Sequoia</a>. All operating systems except tvOS featured <a href="/wiki/Apple_Intelligence" title="Apple Intelligence">Apple Intelligence</a>, Apple’s set of <a href="/wiki/Artificial_intelligence" title="Artificial intelligence">artificial-intelligence</a> apps and services,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-188" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-188"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>188<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-189" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-189"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>189<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> though some of them (such as the “more personalized” <a href="/wiki/Siri" title="Siri">Siri</a>) were later delayed or canceled, leading to a federal lawsuit for <a href="/wiki/False_advertising" title="False advertising">false advertising</a> and <a href="/wiki/Fraud" title="Fraud">fraud</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-190" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-190"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>190<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_event_(September_9,_2024)"><span id="Apple_event_.28September_9.2C_2024.29"></span>Apple event (September 9, 2024)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=141" title="Edit section: Apple event (September 9, 2024)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>An Apple event was held on September 9 at 10:00 am <a href="/wiki/Pacific_Time" class="mw-redirect" title="Pacific Time">Pacific Time</a> with the tagline "It's Glowtime."<sup id="cite&#95;ref-191" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-191"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>191<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Apple unveiled the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_16" title="IPhone 16">iPhone 16</a> with the <a href="/wiki/Apple_silicon" title="Apple silicon">A18 chip</a>, the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_16_Pro" title="IPhone 16 Pro">iPhone 16 Pro</a> with the <a href="/wiki/Apple_silicon" title="Apple silicon">A18 Pro chip</a>, AirPods 4, Apple Watch Series 10 and other products at their event.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-192" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-192"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>192<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2025">2025</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=142" title="Edit section: 2025"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2025_(June_9–13,_2025)"><span id="WWDC_2025_.28June_9.E2.80.9313.2C_2025.29"></span>WWDC 2025 (June 9–13, 2025)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=143" title="Edit section: WWDC 2025 (June 9–13, 2025)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>WWDC 2025 took place online with an in-person experience at Apple Park.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-193" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-193"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>193<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Apple announced <a href="/wiki/IOS_26" title="IOS 26">iOS 26</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPadOS_26" title="IPadOS 26">iPadOS 26</a>, <a href="/wiki/MacOS_Tahoe" title="MacOS Tahoe">macOS 26 Tahoe</a>, <a href="/wiki/WatchOS_26" class="mw-redirect" title="WatchOS 26">watchOS 26</a>, <a href="/wiki/TvOS_26" class="mw-redirect" title="TvOS 26">tvOS 26</a>, and <a href="/wiki/VisionOS_26" class="mw-redirect" title="VisionOS 26">visionOS 26</a>, all featuring a redesign of their <a href="/wiki/User_interface" title="User interface">user interface</a>, titled <a href="/wiki/Liquid_Glass" title="Liquid Glass">Liquid Glass</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-194" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-194"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>194<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> With these updates, Apple switched to a unified naming scheme, using the number of the upcoming year.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_event_(September_9,_2025)"><span id="Apple_event_.28September_9.2C_2025.29"></span>Apple event (September 9, 2025)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=144" title="Edit section: Apple event (September 9, 2025)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>An Apple event was held on September 9 at 10:00 am <a href="/wiki/Pacific_Time" class="mw-redirect" title="Pacific Time">Pacific Time</a> with the tagline "Awe dropping."<sup id="cite&#95;ref-awe&#95;195-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-awe-195"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>195<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The event introduced the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_17" title="IPhone 17">iPhone 17</a>, <a href="/wiki/IPhone_17_Pro" title="IPhone 17 Pro">iPhone 17 Pro and Pro Max</a> as well as a thinner <a href="/wiki/IPhone_Air" title="IPhone Air">iPhone Air</a>, all with a new <a href="/wiki/Apple_A19" title="Apple A19">A19</a> series of chips. Apple also introduced an Apple Watch Series 11 alongside a third-generation Apple Watch SE and Apple Watch Ultra, as well as third-generation AirPods Pro.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-196" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-196"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>196<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-197" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-197"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>197<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="2026">2026</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=145" title="Edit section: 2026"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Apple_event_(March_4,_2026)"><span id="Apple_event_.28March_4.2C_2026.29"></span>Apple event (March 4, 2026)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=146" title="Edit section: Apple event (March 4, 2026)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>An Apple event was held on March 4 at 9:00 am ET, branded as a "special Apple Experience”. The event took place in <a href="/wiki/New_York_City" title="New York City">New York City</a>, <a href="/wiki/London" title="London">London</a> and <a href="/wiki/Shanghai" title="Shanghai">Shanghai</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-198" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-198"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>198<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-199" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-199"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>199<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-200" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-200"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>200<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The event was not live streamed on Apple's website or YouTube channel. The product release took place within the span of three days.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-201" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-201"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>201<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>March 2 2026: Apple announced the <a href="/wiki/IPad_Air_(M4)" title="IPad Air (M4)">M4 iPad Air</a> and the <a href="/wiki/IPhone_17e" title="IPhone 17e">iPhone 17e</a> with MagSafe, <a href="/wiki/Apple_A19" title="Apple A19">A19 chip</a> with 4-core GPU and 256GB starting storage.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-202" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-202"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>202<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li>
<li>March 3 2026: Apple introduced a refreshed <a href="/wiki/Apple_Studio_Display#Second_generation" title="Apple Studio Display">Studio Display</a> and the fully redesigned <a href="/wiki/Studio_Display_XDR" class="mw-redirect" title="Studio Display XDR">Studio Display XDR</a>. The <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Air_(Apple_silicon)" title="MacBook Air (Apple silicon)">MacBook Air</a> was upgraded with the <a href="/wiki/Apple_M5" title="Apple M5">M5</a> chip, and the 14- and 16-inch <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Pro_(Apple_silicon)" title="MacBook Pro (Apple silicon)">MacBook Pro</a> models were upgraded with the <a href="/wiki/Apple_M5" title="Apple M5">M5 Pro</a> and <a href="/wiki/Apple_M5" title="Apple M5">M5 Max</a> chips.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-203" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-203"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>203<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li>
<li>March 4 2026: Apple unveiled the all-new <a href="/wiki/MacBook_Neo" title="MacBook Neo">MacBook Neo</a> with the <a href="/wiki/Apple_A18" title="Apple A18">A18 Pro chip</a> with 5-core GPU, multiple color options, and a 13-inch <a href="/wiki/Liquid_Retina" class="mw-redirect" title="Liquid Retina">Liquid Retina</a> display. However, being an entry-level model, it does not ship with keyboard backlighting, <a href="/wiki/MagSafe" title="MagSafe">MagSafe</a> charging, or any <a href="/wiki/Thunderbolt_(interface)" title="Thunderbolt (interface)">Thunderbolt</a> ports (instead featuring one <a href="/wiki/USB_3.0" title="USB 3.0">USB 3</a> and one <a href="/wiki/USB" title="USB">USB 2</a> ports), and the display is limited to the <a href="/wiki/SRGB" title="SRGB">sRGB</a> gamut instead of <a href="/wiki/Display_P3" class="mw-redirect" title="Display P3">P3</a>. The MacBook Neo starts at $599 ($499 for education) for the 256&#160;GB storage option. The more expensive 512&#160;GB variant includes <a href="/wiki/Touch_ID" title="Touch ID">Touch ID</a> functionality which the 256&#160;GB does not have.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-204" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-204"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>204<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="WWDC_2026_(June_8–12,_2026)"><span id="WWDC_2026_.28June_8.E2.80.9312.2C_2026.29"></span>WWDC 2026 (June 8–12, 2026)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_Apple_Inc._media_events&amp;action=edit&amp;section=147" title="Edit section: WWDC 2026 (June 8–12, 2026)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>WWDC 2026 will take place online with an in-person experience at Apple Park.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-205" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-205"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>205<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Apple is expected to announce iOS 27, iPadOS 27, macOS 27, watchOS 27, tvOS 27 and visionOS 27.
</p>
